- **qdrant-mcp-32602-invalid-request-param-4bddd31dd135f046**: Fixes the Qdrant MCP server showing MCP error -32602 Invalid request parameters in Cursor while working in tests. The cause is Cursor caching a stale connection or schema after the server restarted. The fix is restarting Cursor or reconnecting the server. Use when it works standalone but fails in Cursor; not for Qdrant-side errors. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/qdrant-mcp-32602-invalid-request-param-4bddd31dd135f046))
- **qdrant-mcp-401-unauthorized-set-qdrant-d73596b89b3352c0**: Fixes the Qdrant MCP server getting HTTP 401 Unauthorized from a Qdrant instance that requires an API key. The server sends no key because QDRANT_API_KEY was never set. The fix is setting QDRANT_API_KEY in the client env block. Use when Qdrant requires a key and the server does not send one; not for connection errors. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/qdrant-mcp-401-unauthorized-set-qdrant-d73596b89b3352c0))
- **qdrant-mcp-connection-refused-qdrant-n-c68cd6a801d2d13e**: Fixes the Qdrant MCP server failing with connection refused because Qdrant is not running or QDRANT_URL points at the wrong place. The fix is starting Qdrant and verifying the URL with a curl to /collections. Use when the server cannot reach Qdrant at all; not for auth errors. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/qdrant-mcp-connection-refused-qdrant-n-c68cd6a801d2d13e))
- **qdrant-mcp-not-existing-vector-name-er-d780c4ab0638bb4c**: Fixes the Qdrant MCP server failing to store or search with Not existing vector name error. The collection uses named vectors but the server expects a different vector name (or vice versa). The fix is aligning the server's configured vector name with the collection. Use when the collection exists but store/search fail; not for connection errors. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/qdrant-mcp-not-existing-vector-name-er-d780c4ab0638bb4c))
- **qdrant-mcp-qdrant-url-on-grpc-port-633-753838c5d0fe3b90**: Fixes the Qdrant MCP server failing to connect when QDRANT_URL points at the gRPC port 6334 instead of the REST port 6333. The MCP server talks REST, so the gRPC port never answers correctly. The fix is using port 6333 in QDRANT_URL. Use when the ports are confused; not for auth errors. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/qdrant-mcp-qdrant-url-on-grpc-port-633-753838c5d0fe3b90))

- **qi-read-paper**: 用户给出算法或工程论文，并要求读懂、精读、逐轮讲解、评估复现，或把论文和代码对照着读时使用：分三轮讲清问题、方法、图表证据、局限和复现，给了代码时加一轮代码对照。默认逐轮对话；用户要网页时一次读完，生成内嵌原图和解释图的单文件网页。只问一个可直接回答的具体问题时直接回答。不用于撰写技术文档、改文学风格，或从零实现论文（paper-to-code）。 ([nerdneilsfield/skills](https://github.com/nerdneilsfield/skills/tree/HEAD/research/qi-read-paper))

- **qian-li-ming-gao**: 用户点名《千里命稿》或韦千里，或要做八步评断、命例对照时用本 skill；问病药体系优先 shen-feng-tong-kao，问月令顺逆与相神优先 ming-li-tan-yuan，问量化旺衰优先 si-zhu-bazi-jichu。《千里命稿》子平八字评断体系：适用于八字评断全流程、八步评断与命例对照；未点名韦千里或未要求八步/报告时，泛问整体优先 san-ming-tong-hui 或 yuan-hai-zi-ping，以及格局成败、用神、大运流年、六亲事业疾病的实战判断；不适用于排盘起运教学（本书有起例但不系统）、择日、风水。Keywords: bazi, four pillars, Chinese astrology, 八字, 四柱, 格局, 用神, 大运, 流年, 六亲. ([linshibo1994/chinese-metaphysics-skills](https://github.com/linshibo1994/chinese-metaphysics-skills/tree/HEAD/qian-li-ming-gao))
- **qian-li-qun-perspective**: 钱理群视角：以鲁迅精神为核心的知识分子精神史研究与教育批判框架，基于《心灵的探寻》《与鲁迅相遇》《1948：天地玄黄》等第一手资料构建。 触发词：「钱理群」「鲁迅」「精致的利己主义者」「知识分子精神史」「民间思想史」「北大精神」「精神界战士」「二十世纪中国文学」。 English triggers: "Qian Liqun", "Lu Xun", "intellectual history", "spiritual warrior", "Peking University spirit", "Chinese modern literature". ([guolios/xianzheng_skills](https://github.com/guolios/xianzheng_skills/tree/HEAD/%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E5%AE%B6skills/%E9%92%B1%E7%90%86%E7%BE%A4Skill) · ★7)
- **qian-long-liu-jie-duan**: 何时调用：当用户需要理解从潜龙到亢龙的完整发展曲线时。 关键信号："怎么分析发展阶段？"、"潜龙勿用到亢龙有悔？"、"生命周期模型？"。 何时不调用：用户需要的是三才之道（用 san-cai-zhi-dao），或用户需要的是时位中正（用 shi-wei-zhong-zheng）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/zhou-yi/qian-long-liu-jie-duan) · ★1)
- **qian-meng-long-perspective**: 钱梦龙视角：以"三主四式"为核心的语文导读法框架，基于《我和语文导读法》《导读的艺术》等第一手资料构建。 触发词：「钱梦龙」「语文导读法」「三主」「四式」「学生为主体」「教师为主导」「训练为主线」「自读」「教读」「复读」。 English triggers: "Qian Menglong", "Chinese reading guidance method", "three principles", "four modes", "student-centered", "teacher-guided", "training-focused". - **qian-mu-context-skill**: 把零散历史事件放回政治社会经济学术脉络或比较不同时期制度时使用。提供研究意义、通史关联、制度与文化的情境解释的具体工作流，区分资料依据与现代转译，输出可复核的分析与行动。 ([constantin2088/qian-mu-context-skill](https://github.com/constantin2088/qian-mu-context-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
